/home/snowleopard/src/reference-Kernel-3188-X7/arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-4- *  Special dma_{map/unmap/dma_sync}_* routines for systems that have
/home/snowleopard/src/reference-Kernel-3188-X7/arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-5- *  limited DMA windows. These functions utilize bounce buffers to
/home/snowleopard/src/reference-Kernel-3188-X7/arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-6- *  copy data to/from buffers located outside the DMA region. This
/home/snowleopard/src/reference-Kernel-3188-X7/arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-7- *  only works for systems in which DMA memory is at the bottom of
/home/snowleopard/src/reference-Kernel-3188-X7/arch/arm/common/dmabounce.c-8- *  RAM, the remainder of memory is at the top and the DMA memory

========== CURRENT CARVEOUT PAGE MANAGEMENT ==========
// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0
/*
 * Rockchip legacy ION carveout heap.
 */

#include <linux/err.h>
#include <linux/genalloc.h>
#include <linux/init.h>
#include <linux/kernel.h>
#include <linux/log2.h>
#include <linux/mm.h>
#include <linux/of.h>
#include <linux/of_address.h>
#include <linux/of_reserved_mem.h>
#include <linux/overflow.h>
#include <linux/resource.h>
#include <linux/scatterlist.h>
#include <linux/seq_file.h>
#include <linux/slab.h>
#include <linux/spinlock.h>

#include "ion.h"

#define ION_CARVEOUT_ALLOCATE_FAIL   ((phys_addr_t)-1)
#define ION_NORHEAP_COMPATIBLE       "rockchip,rk3188-ion-norheap"
#define ION_NORHEAP_NAME             "norheap"

struct ion_carveout_heap {
        struct ion_heap heap;
        struct gen_pool *pool;
        phys_addr_t base;
        size_t size;
        spinlock_t stats_lock;
        size_t allocated_size;
        size_t max_allocated;
};

static phys_addr_t ion_carveout_allocate(struct ion_heap *heap,
                                         unsigned long size,
                                         unsigned long align)
{
        struct ion_carveout_heap *carveout_heap =
                container_of(heap, struct ion_carveout_heap, heap);
        struct genpool_data_align data;
        unsigned long addr;
        unsigned long irqflags;

        if (align < PAGE_SIZE)
                align = PAGE_SIZE;

        if (!is_power_of_2(align) || align > INT_MAX)
                return ION_CARVEOUT_ALLOCATE_FAIL;

        data.align = (int)align;

        addr = gen_pool_alloc_algo(carveout_heap->pool, size,
                                   gen_pool_first_fit_align, &data);
        if (!addr)
                return ION_CARVEOUT_ALLOCATE_FAIL;

        spin_lock_irqsave(&carveout_heap->stats_lock, irqflags);
        carveout_heap->allocated_size += size;
        if (carveout_heap->allocated_size > carveout_heap->max_allocated)
                carveout_heap->max_allocated = carveout_heap->allocated_size;
        spin_unlock_irqrestore(&carveout_heap->stats_lock, irqflags);

        return (phys_addr_t)addr;
}

static void ion_carveout_free(struct ion_heap *heap, phys_addr_t addr,
                              unsigned long size)
{
        struct ion_carveout_heap *carveout_heap =
                container_of(heap, struct ion_carveout_heap, heap);
        unsigned long irqflags;

        if (addr == ION_CARVEOUT_ALLOCATE_FAIL || !size)
                return;

        spin_lock_irqsave(&carveout_heap->stats_lock, irqflags);
        if (WARN_ON(carveout_heap->allocated_size < size))
                carveout_heap->allocated_size = 0;
        else
                carveout_heap->allocated_size -= size;
        spin_unlock_irqrestore(&carveout_heap->stats_lock, irqflags);

        gen_pool_free(carveout_heap->pool, (unsigned long)addr, size);
}

static int ion_carveout_heap_allocate(struct ion_heap *heap,
                                      struct ion_buffer *buffer,
                                      unsigned long size,
                                      unsigned long flags)
{
        struct sg_table *table;
        phys_addr_t paddr;
        int ret;

        table = kmalloc(sizeof(*table), GFP_KERNEL);
        if (!table)
                return -ENOMEM;

        ret = sg_alloc_table(table, 1, GFP_KERNEL);
        if (ret)
                goto err_free;

        paddr = ion_carveout_allocate(heap, size, buffer->align);
        if (paddr == ION_CARVEOUT_ALLOCATE_FAIL) {
                ret = -ENOMEM;
                goto err_free_table;
        }

        sg_set_page(table->sgl, pfn_to_page(PFN_DOWN(paddr)), size, 0);
        buffer->sg_table = table;

        return 0;

err_free_table:
        sg_free_table(table);
err_free:
        kfree(table);
        return ret;
}

static void ion_carveout_heap_free(struct ion_buffer *buffer)
{
        struct ion_heap *heap = buffer->heap;
        struct sg_table *table = buffer->sg_table;
        phys_addr_t paddr;

        if (WARN_ON(!table))
                return;

        paddr = sg_phys(table->sgl);

        if (ion_heap_buffer_zero(buffer))
                pr_warn_ratelimited("ion: failed to zero norheap buffer\n");

        ion_carveout_free(heap, paddr, buffer->size);

        sg_free_table(table);
        kfree(table);
}

static int ion_carveout_heap_debug_show(struct ion_heap *heap,
                                        struct seq_file *s,
                                        void *unused)
{
        struct ion_carveout_heap *carveout_heap =
                container_of(heap, struct ion_carveout_heap, heap);
        size_t total = gen_pool_size(carveout_heap->pool);
        size_t available = gen_pool_avail(carveout_heap->pool);

        seq_printf(s, "Base:      0x%pa\n", &carveout_heap->base);
        seq_printf(s, "Total:     %zu bytes\n", total);
        seq_printf(s, "Available: %zu bytes\n", available);
        seq_printf(s, "Allocated: %zu bytes\n", total - available);

        return 0;
}

static struct ion_heap_ops carveout_heap_ops = {
        .allocate = ion_carveout_heap_allocate,
        .free = ion_carveout_heap_free,
        .map_user = ion_heap_map_user,
        .map_kernel = ion_heap_map_kernel,
        .unmap_kernel = ion_heap_unmap_kernel,
};

struct ion_heap *ion_carveout_heap_create(struct ion_platform_heap *heap_data)
{
        struct ion_carveout_heap *carveout_heap;
        struct page *page;
        phys_addr_t end;
        unsigned long first_pfn, last_pfn, pfn;
        int ret;

        if (!heap_data || !heap_data->size)
                return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL);

        if (!IS_ALIGNED(heap_data->base, PAGE_SIZE) ||
            !IS_ALIGNED(heap_data->size, PAGE_SIZE))
                return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL);

        if (check_add_overflow(heap_data->base,
                               (phys_addr_t)heap_data->size, &end))
                return ERR_PTR(-EOVERFLOW);

        if (end <= heap_data->base || heap_data->base > ULONG_MAX)
                return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL);

        /*
         * The reserved region must retain valid struct page descriptors.
         * Do not mark it "no-map" in the device tree.
         */
        first_pfn = PFN_DOWN(heap_data->base);
        last_pfn = PFN_DOWN(end - 1);

        for (pfn = first_pfn; ; pfn++) {
                if (!pfn_valid(pfn)) {
                        pr_err("ion: norheap contains invalid PFN 0x%lx\n",
                               pfn);
                        return ERR_PTR(-EINVAL);
                }

                if (pfn == last_pfn)
                        break;
        }

        carveout_heap = kzalloc(sizeof(*carveout_heap), GFP_KERNEL);
        if (!carveout_heap)
                return ERR_PTR(-ENOMEM);

        spin_lock_init(&carveout_heap->stats_lock);
        carveout_heap->allocated_size = 0;
        carveout_heap->max_allocated = 0;

        carveout_heap->pool = gen_pool_create(PAGE_SHIFT, -1);
        if (!carveout_heap->pool) {
                ret = -ENOMEM;
                goto err_free_heap;
        }

        carveout_heap->base = heap_data->base;
        carveout_heap->size = heap_data->size;

        ret = gen_pool_add(carveout_heap->pool,
                           (unsigned long)heap_data->base,
                           heap_data->size, -1);
        if (ret)
                goto err_destroy_pool;

        page = pfn_to_page(first_pfn);

        ret = ion_heap_pages_zero(page, heap_data->size,
                                  pgprot_writecombine(PAGE_KERNEL));
        if (ret)
                goto err_destroy_pool;

        carveout_heap->heap.ops = &carveout_heap_ops;
        carveout_heap->heap.type = ION_HEAP_TYPE_CARVEOUT;
        carveout_heap->heap.flags = 0;
        carveout_heap->heap.name = heap_data->name ?
                                   heap_data->name : ION_NORHEAP_NAME;
        carveout_heap->heap.debug_show = ion_carveout_heap_debug_show;

        return &carveout_heap->heap;

err_destroy_pool:
        gen_pool_destroy(carveout_heap->pool);
err_free_heap:
        kfree(carveout_heap);
        return ERR_PTR(ret);
}

int ion_carveout_heap_get_stats(struct ion_heap *heap, size_t *allocated,
                                size_t *max_allocated, size_t *total)
{
        struct ion_carveout_heap *carveout_heap;
        unsigned long irqflags;

        if (!heap || heap->type != ION_HEAP_TYPE_CARVEOUT ||
            !allocated || !max_allocated || !total)
                return -EINVAL;

        carveout_heap = container_of(heap, struct ion_carveout_heap, heap);
        spin_lock_irqsave(&carveout_heap->stats_lock, irqflags);
        *allocated = carveout_heap->allocated_size;
        *max_allocated = carveout_heap->max_allocated;
        *total = carveout_heap->size;
        spin_unlock_irqrestore(&carveout_heap->stats_lock, irqflags);

        return 0;
}

int ion_add_carveout_heap(void)
{
        struct device_node *np;
        struct reserved_mem *rmem;
        struct resource res;
        struct ion_platform_heap heap_data = {
                .type = ION_HEAP_TYPE_CARVEOUT,
                .id = 0,
                .name = ION_NORHEAP_NAME,
                .align = PAGE_SIZE,
        };
        struct ion_heap *heap;
        int ret;

        np = of_find_compatible_node(NULL, NULL, ION_NORHEAP_COMPATIBLE);
        if (!np) {
                pr_info("ion: no %s reserved-memory node; norheap skipped\n",
                        ION_NORHEAP_COMPATIBLE);
                return 0;
        }

        if (!of_device_is_available(np)) {
                pr_info("ion: norheap node is disabled\n");
                ret = 0;
                goto out_node;
        }

        ret = of_address_to_resource(np, 0, &res);
        if (ret) {
                pr_err("ion: cannot read norheap resource: %d\n", ret);
                goto out_node;
        }

        rmem = of_reserved_mem_lookup(np);
        if (!rmem) {
                pr_err("ion: norheap node is not registered as reserved memory\n");
                ret = -EINVAL;
                goto out_node;
        }

        if (rmem->base != res.start ||
            rmem->size != resource_size(&res)) {
                pr_err("ion: norheap resource and reserved-memory data disagree\n");
                ret = -EINVAL;
                goto out_node;
        }

        heap_data.base = rmem->base;
        heap_data.size = rmem->size;

        pr_info("ion: creating norheap at 0x%pa size 0x%zx\n",
                &heap_data.base, heap_data.size);

        heap = ion_carveout_heap_create(&heap_data);
        if (IS_ERR(heap)) {
                ret = PTR_ERR(heap);
                pr_err("ion: norheap creation failed: %d\n", ret);
                goto out_node;
        }

        ion_device_add_heap(heap);
        pr_info("ion: norheap registered\n");
        ret = 0;

out_node:
        of_node_put(np);
        return ret;
}

#ifndef CONFIG_ION_MODULE
device_initcall(ion_add_carveout_heap);
#endif
